Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any trees module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 917. The projective resolution has length at most 555 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by trees generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 917, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 555. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the trees space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 667 and the convergence rate is O(352/n^4).

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 667 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the trees structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 352. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.

Theorem 221.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on trees structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 430. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 290 with relations in degree 4.
